SalesOn API
The Saleson API allows you to perform all the operations that you do with our web client. SalesOn API is built using REST principles which ensures predictable URLs that makes writing applications easy.

Overview
The SalesOn API likely refers to APIs used to integrate the SalesOn field sales management software with other systems, such as ERP software, for purposes like managing sales orders, tracking salesman location, and handling invoicing. The SalesOn platform itself uses APIs for its mobile app and to connect to other business tools.

Company Id
In SalesOn, your business is termed as an company. If you have multiple businesses, you simply set each of those up as an individual company. Each company is an independent SalesOn company with it’s own company ID, base currency, time zone, language, contacts, reports, etc.

The parameter company_id along with the company_id should be sent in with every API request to identify the company.

The company_id can be obtained from the GET /company API’s JSON response.

API Call Limit
API calls are limited to provide better quality of service and availability to all the users. You can make 100 requests per minute per organisation. The limits on total requests per day are listed below for each plan:

Free Plan - 1000 API requests/day

Standard Plan- 2000 requests/day

Professional Plan- 5000 requests/day

Premium Plan- 10000 requests/day

Elite Plan- 10000 requests/day

Ultimate Plan- 10000 requests/day

POST
Update Sell Price
{{host}}products
Update Selling Price
This endpoint is used to update the selling price

Arguments
sell_price(number): The selling price of product

party_id (number): The party id for which the product stock to be adjusted

Request
Method :POST

URL:{{host}}products/13500/update-sell-price

{ "sell_price": 900, "party_id": 10482}

Response
The response will contain the updated selling price of the Product
AUTHORIZATION
Bearer Token
Token
{{token}}

keep-alive

application/json

?0

curl
curl --location -g '{{host}}products' \
--header 'Connection: keep-alive' \
--header 'sec-ch-ua: "Chromium";v="94", "Google Chrome";v="94", ";Not A Brand";v="99"' \
--header 'Accept: application/json, text/javascript, */*; q=0.01' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer SXZBTTNyNzFndzJCVVBBNjB2NnpFY1MwZVozeFdyZDdveEVZSnkxbzEwMDAx' \
--header 'sec-ch-ua-mobile: ?0' \
--header 'User-Agent: Mozilla/5.0 (Macintosh; Intel Mac OS X 10_14_6) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/94.0.4606.61 Safari/537.36' \
--header 'sec-ch-ua-platform: "macOS"' \
--header 'Origin: http://localhost:7171' \
--header 'Sec-Fetch-Site: same-site' \
--header 'Sec-Fetch-Mode: cors' \
--header 'Sec-Fetch-Dest: empty' \
--header 'Referer: http://localhost:7171/' \
--header 'Accept-Language: en-GB,en-US;q=0.9,en;q=0.8' \
--data '{
    "sell_price": 900,  
    "party_id": 10482
}'

POST
Create Party
{{host}}parties
Create Party
This endpoint allows to create the party.

Arguments:
gstin()
The gst to be applied on the product

opening_balance():
The oprning balance of the party

mobile():
The mobile number of party

email():
The email address of the party holder

type()
The type of party eg. customer, retailer etc.

credit_period(number):
The time given for the credit for party to be paid

credit_limit(number):
The limit of credit of party for sales invoice

name(string)
The name of party

discount(integer)
The discount to be applied on sales invoice for the party

Request
Method:POST

URL:{{host}}parties

{ "name":"Narayani Entessrurises", "mobile":"9199979570", "email": "suraj@saleson.co.in", "type": "Retailer", "gstin": "29APVPJ29811ZA", "credit_period": 0, "credit_limit": 100, "opening_balacne": 0, "discount": 5}

GET
Get Invoices Detail
{{host}}transactions/sales-invoice/3725
Get Invoices Detail
This end point allows you to get specified invoice/transaction detail according to provided id

Attributes
party_id():
The unique identifier of party

paid():
This field is for whether transaction is paid or not

created_by():
The created_by field shows that from whom itransaction is created.

Request
Method:GET

URL:{{host}}transactions/sales-invoice/3714

{"party_id": 11558, "paid":0, "created_by":10549}

Response
The JSON schema for this request will get you the transaction with specified id

AUTHORIZATION
Bearer Token
Token
{{token}}

HEADERS
Content-Type
application/json

Body
raw (json)
json
{ 
	"party_id": 11558,
	"paid":0,
    "created_by":10549
}

POST
Create transaction in Bulk
{{host}}transactions/sales-invoice/bulk
Create Transaction in bulk
This end point allows you to create transaction according to provided id

Attributes
type():

The type of transactions

id():

The unique identifier of party

Request
Method:POST

URL:{{host}}transactions/sales-invoice/bulk

{type": "sales-order", "ids":[116780,116862]}

Response
The response for this request is documented as a JSON schema with  "status": "Success",   "message": "Sales Invoice created successfully

AUTHORIZATION
Bearer Token
Token
{{token}}

HEADERS
Content-Type
application/json

Body
raw (json)
json
{"type":"Sales Invoice","ids":[116780,116862]}
Example Request
Create transaction in Bulk
curl
curl --location -g '{{host}}transactions/sales-invoice/bulk' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{"type":"Sales Order","ids":[116780,116862]}'
200 OK
Example Response
Body
Headers (10)
json
{
  "status": "Success",
  "message": "Sales Order created successfully"
}

PUT
Update Transactions
{{host}}transactions/sales-invoice/3718
Update Transaction
This endpoint allows to update the transactions

Attributes
type():The type of transaction to be created eg. sales invoice ,sales order etc.

party(object) :

party_id(integer) The id of party

name(string):The mobile number of an party holder

mobile(integer):The multiplier of the pri unit

address(string):The address of party located

products(object):

free_qty(): The quantity of product which given free to party in sales

gst(): The gst tax applied on product

hsn() : The hsn number of product

name(): The name of product

product_id():The product id is a unique identifier to identify the product

quantity(): The qunatty is the number of product added in sales

sell_price(): The selling price is a price to sale the product

unit():The Unit of product in which it is measured

Request
Method :POST

URL:{{host}}transactions/sales-invoice

{ "party" : { "party_id": 11558, "address": "Nehru Road", "mobile": "9876543210", "name" : "Sample Trader" },
"products": [ { "free_qty": 0, "gst": 5, "hsn": "9603", "name": "Aashirwad atta1", "product_id": 19683, "quantity": "1", "sell_price": 50, "unit": "Gms" } ], "type": "Sales Invoice"}

Response
The response of this request is a JSON schema containing the information of updated transaction

This request doesn't return any response body
Payment
Payment refers to the act of giving money or transferring value in exchange for goods, services, or to fulfill a legal obligation. It can involve various forms, including cash, checks, credit cards, debit cards, bank transfers, and electronic payments

Attributes
party_id(Integer):

Unique ID of the party generated by the server for whom payment to be raised

cheque_date(string) :

The date on which credit note is raised. Format [yyyy-mm-dd]

type(string)

The patment type to be created . example- CREDIT
amount(integer):

Payment amount made to the Company

mode(string)

Mode of Vendor Payment. Example -CASH, ONLINE or CHEQUE

Status(string):

The status of payment made ie payment is in pending status or proved or rejected

comment(string):

The additional infoemation is added in comment section

payment_details(object):Individual payment details

invoice_id(integer): ID of the invoice. Search payments by invoice or invoice name

amount(integer): The amount of invoice that has to be paid to company

company_id(integer):The id of the company

payment _id(integer):The id of payment that to be created

created_at(): The date and time of payment creation

updated_at(): The date and time of payment updation

inv_no(); The invoice number for which payment to be created

inv_due_date(): The last date of invoice

inv_created_at(): The creation of invoice is made at

inv_amount(): The total amount of invoice

attachment(): Any attachment to payment

page_size()

The response is stored in the number of page is the page size.

start_date()

The start date of the payment.

end_date()

The end date of payment. ie the last day ti make patment

Payment_ids():

The payment id is a unique identifier using which you can update payment status in bulk

End points
POST /payments
GET payments/credit/payment_id
GET /payments/credit
GET /payments/debit
POSTpayments/payment_id
DELETE /payments/credit/payment_id
GET /payments/credit/html?ids
PATCH /payments/credit/bulk-status
GET /payments/credit/view/public_id

Dashboard
Overview
A Saleson dashboard is a freature that provides a visual overview of key sales metrics and data, often in the form of graphs and charts. It's designed to give sales teams and managers a quick, at-a-glance view of their performance and progress toward goals.

End points
GET/dashboard/statistics
GET/dashboard/transactional-stats
GET/dashboard/top-sales-by-entity
GET/dashboard/total-sales-by-entity

Van Sale
Overview
Van sale is a sales and delivery method where a sales representative, often in a van, sells and delivers products directly to retailers or customers

Van sales in a saleson is a module designed to streamline and enhance the process of managing sales and delivery operations for businesses that utilize vans to reach customers.This module often include features for order management, payment collection, inventory tracking, route planning, and reporting. Essentially, they are tools that empower sales representatives and drivers to perform tasks like taking orders, managing inventory, and collecting payments directly from customers while on the road.

End point
POST /van-sales
PUT /van-sales/van-sales-id
PATCH /van-sales/van-sales-id/status
GET /van-sales
GET /van-sales/van-sales-id
DELETE /van-sales/van-sales-id

POST
Here Map Sequencing API POST
https://wps.hereapi.com/v8/findsequence2
Find Sequence API
This endpoint allows users to find a sequence of waypoints based on provided parameters. It is particularly useful for applications that require route optimization or planning based on specific waypoints.

Arguments
mode(boolean):

The mode of the route to be enables or disabled

start(string):

The start point of route

destination(string):

the destination of the route

end(string):

The end of the route

departure(string):

The departure time of the vehical

AUTHORIZATION

apiKey

Value
<value>

destination3

destination4

end

Example Request
Here Map Sequencing API POST
curl
curl --location 'https://wps.hereapi.com/v8/findsequence2' \
--form 'mode="fastest;car;traffic:enabled"' \
--form 'start="WiesbadenCentralStation;50.0715,8.2434"' \
--form 'destination1="FranfurtCentralStation;50.1073,8.6647"' \
--form 'destination2="DarmstadtCentralStation;49.8728,8.6326"' \
--form 'destination4="HanauCentralStation;50.1218,8.9298"' \
--form 'destination3="FrankfurtAirport;50.0505,8.5698"' \
--form 'end="MainzCentralStation;50.0021,8.259"' \
--form 'improveFor="time"' \
--form 'departure="2014-12-09T09:30:00+01:00"'
200 OK

Authentication
In saleon the user needs authentication to use the resourses. In saleson

Token-based authentication is used (e.g., smart cards, one-time passwords) for verification.

POST
Get OTP
{{host}}users/getOTP
GET OTP
This endpoint allows user to GET One Time Password

Arguments
mobile(integer)

The Users mobile number to send otp

HEADERS
Content-Type
application/json

Body
raw (json)
json
{
	"mobile" : {{mobile}}
}
Example Request
Get OTP
curl
curl --location -g '{{host}}users/getOTP' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--data '{
	"mobile" : {{mobile}}
}'
200 OK
Example Response
Body
Headers (10)
json
{
  "message": "OTP sent successfully!"
}

Setup Account
This endpoint allows users to setup their account as per their requirements

Arguments

The name of company.

mobile():

The users mobile number

email():

The users email address.

The type of company.

name():

The name of user.

password():

The password for users account.

application/json

Bearer {{token}}

	"company_name":"Balaji Motors",

    "password": "12345"
}
